1 confirmed_leak src/3rdParty/salomesmesh/src/SMDS/SMDS_UnstructuredGrid.cpp:489:45

The grid owns the allocation through a raw pointer, but grid destruction performs no deletion and the mesh destructor does not invoke the explicit cleanup path.
- src/3rdParty/salomesmesh/src/SMDS/SMDS_UnstructuredGrid.cpp:489 allocates SMDS_DownQuadPenta and stores it in raw-pointer _downArray.
- src/3rdParty/salomesmesh/src/SMDS/SMDS_UnstructuredGrid.cpp:453 deletes _downArray entries only when CleanDownwardConnectivity() is explicitly called.
- src/3rdParty/salomesmesh/src/SMDS/SMDS_UnstructuredGrid.cpp:82-84 has an empty SMDS_UnstructuredGrid destructor, and src/3rdParty/salomesmesh/src/SMDS/SMDS_Mesh.cpp:2725 deletes the grid without calling cleanup.
